Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any complex_analysis module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 557. The projective resolution has length at most 797 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the complex_analysis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 557 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 797. QED.
